### [DIVISION XV.2]{.HLabel2}[Leave of Absence for Members of the Reserve Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-343278 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to leave

- **[[247.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employee who is a member of the
  reserve force and has completed at least three consecutive months of
  continuous employment with an employer --- or a shorter period that is
  prescribed for a class of employees to which the employee belongs ---
  is entitled to and shall be granted a leave of absence from employment
  to take part in the following operations or activities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an operation in Canada or abroad --- including
    preparation, training, rest or travel from or to the employee's
    residence --- that is designated by the Minister of National
    Defence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an activity set out in the regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Canadian Armed Forces military skills training;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} training that they are ordered to take under
    paragraph 33(2)(a) of the [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} duties that they are called out on service to
    perform under paragraph 33(2)(b) of the [National Defence
    Act](/eng/acts/N-5);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} service in aid of a civil power for which they are
    called out under section 275 of the [National Defence
    Act](/eng/acts/N-5); or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} treatment, recovery or rehabilitation in respect of
    a physical or mental health problem that results from service in an
    operation or activity referred to in this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum of 24 months

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Leaves taken by an employee under paragraphs (1)(a)
  to (d) can total no more than an aggregate of 24 months in any
  60-month period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1.1) does not apply to a leave of
  absence taken as a result of a [[national
  emergency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, within the meaning of the
  [Emergencies Act](/eng/acts/E-4.5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation and delegation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of National Defence may designate an
  operation for the purposes of paragraph (1)(a) or may authorize
  another person to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A designation takes effect on the day on which it is
  made or on an earlier or later day that is fixed by the Minister of
  National Defence or the other person. The Minister of National Defence
  or the other person may fix the day on which the designation ceases to
  be in effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an employee is not entitled
  to a leave of absence under this Division if, in the opinion of the
  Head, it would adversely affect public health or safety or would cause
  undue hardship to the employer if the employee, as an individual or as
  a member of a class of employees, were to take leave.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
- [2018, c. 27, s. 494](#1039976-1201191){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1039976-1201191.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 584](#1040498-1282180){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040498-1282180.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-247.5-20190901.html "Link to previous version of section 247.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to employer

- **[[247.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employee who takes a leave of
  absence under this Division shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless there is a valid reason for not doing so,
    give at least four weeks' notice to the employer before the day on
    which the leave is to begin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inform the employer of the length of the leave.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If there is a valid reason

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there is a valid reason for not providing notice
  in accordance with paragraph (1)(a), the employee shall notify the
  employer as soon as practicable that the employee is taking a leave of
  absence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change in length of leave

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless there is a valid reason for not doing so, an
  employee who takes a leave of absence under this Division shall notify
  the employer of any change in the length of the leave at least four
  weeks before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the new day on which the leave is to end, if the
    employee is taking a shorter leave; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day that was most recently indicated for the
    leave to end, if the employee is taking a longer leave.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}In writing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless there is a valid reason for not doing so, any
  notice or other information to be provided by the employee to the
  employer under this section is to be in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for proof

- **[[247.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if the
  employer requests proof that a leave of absence is taken under this
  Division, the employee shall provide the employer with the prescribed
  document, if any, or with a document that is approved by the Chief of
  the Defence Staff who was appointed under subsection 18(1) of the
  [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No prescribed or approved document

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If no document is prescribed, or approved by the
  Chief of the Defence Staff, the employee shall, on request, provide
  the employer with a document from the employee's commanding officer
  specifying that the employee is taking part in an operation or
  activity referred to in paragraphs 247.5(1)(a) to (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless there is a valid reason for not doing so, the
  employee shall provide the document referred to in subsection (1)
  or (2) within three weeks after the day on which the leave begins.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return to work postponed

- **[[247.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the employee does not notify the
  employer at least four weeks before the day on which the leave that is
  taken under this Division is to end, the employer may postpone the
  employee's return to work for a period of up to four weeks after the
  day on which the employee informs the employer of the end date of the
  leave. If the employer informs the employee that their return to work
  is postponed, the employee is not entitled to return to work until the
  day that is indicated by the employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection (1) does not apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the employee
  notifies the employer in accordance with paragraph 247.6(1)(b) before
  the leave begins and if the length of the leave is not changed after
  the leave begins.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed part of leave

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The period of postponement referred to in
  subsection (1) is deemed to be part of the leave.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

**[[247.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.9 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2017,
c. 33, s. 211\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-247.9-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 247.9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuous employment --- benefits

- **[[247.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of calculating the
  benefits of an employee who takes a leave of absence under this
  Division, employment on the employee's return to work is deemed to be
  continuous with their employment before the leave.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seniority

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The seniority of an employee who takes a leave of
  absence under this Division shall accumulate during the leave.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 189

**[[247.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.92 .sectionLabel}** Section 189
applies for the purposes of this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resumption of employment in same position

- **[[247.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.93
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At the end of a leave of absence
  that is taken under this Division, the employer shall reinstate the
  employee in the position that the employee occupied on the day before
  the day on which the leave begins.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comparable position

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If for a valid reason an employer is not able to
  reinstate the employee in that position, they shall reinstate the
  employee in a comparable position with the same wages and benefits and
  in the same location.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not able to perform work

**[[247.94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.94 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, if an employee is not able to perform the functions of the
position that they occupy before the leave begins --- or those of a
comparable position, with the same wages and benefits and in the same
location --- the employer may assign them to a position with different
terms or conditions of employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wages or benefits affected by
reorganization

- **[[247.95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.95
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, during a leave of absence that
  is taken under this Division, the wages or benefits of the group of
  employees of which an employee is a member are changed as part of a
  plan to reorganize the industrial establishment in which that group is
  employed, the employee is entitled, on reinstatement under this
  section, to receive the wages and benefits in respect of that
  employment that that employee would have been entitled to receive had
  that employee been working when the reorganization took place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change in wages or benefits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer of an employee who is on leave and whose
  wages or benefits would be changed as a result of the reorganization
  shall, as soon as practicable, send a notice to the employee at their
  last known address.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- employee

- **[[247.96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.96
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No employer may dismiss, suspend,
  lay off, demote or discipline an employee because they are a member of
  the reserve force or intend to take or have taken a leave of absence
  under this Division or take into account the fact that an employee is
  a member of the reserve force or intends to take or has taken a leave
  of absence under this Division in a decision to promote or train them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- future employee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person may refuse to employ a person because they
  are a member of the reserve force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[247.97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.97 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations for carrying out the purposes of this
Division and, without restricting the generality of the foregoing, may
make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} specifying the absences that are deemed not to
  interrupt continuity of employment for the purpose of subsection
  247.5(1);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying what constitutes or does not constitute an
  operation for the purposes of paragraph 247.5(1)(a);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} setting out the activities for the purposes of
  paragraph 247.5(1)(b);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} defining "military skills training" for the purposes
  of paragraph 247.5(1)(c);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} limiting the duration of the treatment, recovery or
  rehabilitation referred to in paragraph 247.5(1)(g) or setting out the
  terms or conditions for the application of that paragraph;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} specifying what constitutes or does not constitute
  undue hardship for the purposes of subsection 247.5(4);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} specifying what constitutes or does not constitute a
  valid reason for the purposes of subsection 247.6(1), (2), (3) or (4),
  247.7(3) or 247.93(2);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances in which section 247.7,
  subsection 247.8(1) or subsection 247.91(2) does not apply;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} specifying the circumstances in which an employer may
  not assign an employee to a position with different terms or
  conditions of employment for the purposes of section 247.94;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 27, s. 495\]]{.Repealed}

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 27, s. 495\]]{.Repealed}

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the classes of employees that are not
  entitled to a leave of absence under this Division if the Governor in
  Council is satisfied that the fact of taking leave would cause
  unreasonable consequences; and

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances in which classes of
  employees are not entitled to a leave of absence under this Division.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 15, s. 1
- [2017, c. 33, s. 212](#1037458-1200942){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037458-1200942.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 495](#1039989-1201195){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1039989-1201195.html"}
:::

:::

### [DIVISION XV.3]{.HLabel2}[Genetic Testing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-343387 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[247.98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.98
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Division.

  [[disclose]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disclose]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes to authorize
      disclosure. ([communiquer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[genetic test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[genetic test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an employee,
      means a test that analyzes the employee's DNA, RNA or chromosomes
      for purposes such as the prediction of disease or vertical
      transmission risks, or monitoring, diagnosis or prognosis. ([test
      génétique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Genetic test

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every employee is entitled not to undergo or be
  required to undergo a genetic test.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of results

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every employee is entitled not to disclose or be
  required to disclose the results of a genetic test.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disciplinary action

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No employer shall dismiss, suspend, lay off or demote
  an employee, impose a financial or other penalty on an employee, or
  refuse to pay an employee remuneration in respect of any period that
  the employee would, but for the exercise of the employee's rights
  under this Division, have worked, or take any disciplinary action
  against or threaten to take any such action against an employee

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} because the employee refused a request by the
    employer to undergo a genetic test;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} because the employee refused to disclose the
    results of a genetic test; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} on the basis of the results of a genetic test
    undergone by the employee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by third party

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall disclose to an employer that an
  employee has undergone a genetic test, or disclose to an employer the
  results of a genetic test, without the written consent of the
  employee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Collection or use

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No employer shall collect or use the results of a
  genetic test without the written consent of the employee who has
  undergone the test.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 3, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaint

- **[[247.99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.99
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employee who alleges that an
  employer has taken action against the employee in contravention of
  subsection 247.98(4) may make a complaint in writing to the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the fact that an employer
  complies with Divisions X and XI does not

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} affect the employee's rights under this Division;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent the Board from deciding under paragraph
    (7)(a) that the employer has taken action against the employee in
    contravention of subsection 247.98(4) or from making any order under
    subsection (8), including a reinstatement order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for making complaint

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the complaint shall be
  made to the Head not later than 90 days after the day on which the
  complainant knew, or in the Head's opinion ought to have known, of the
  action or circumstances giving rise to the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Head may extend the period of time referred to in
  subsection (2)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Head is satisfied that a complaint was made
    in that period to a government official who had no authority to deal
    with the complaint but that the employee making the complaint
    believed the official had that authority; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other circumstance that is prescribed by
    regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head to assist parties

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a complaint made under subsection (1),
  the Head shall endeavour to assist the parties to the complaint to
  settle the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaint not settled within reasonable
  time

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a complaint is not settled under subsection (4)
  within the period that the Head considers to be reasonable in the
  circumstances, the Head must, on the written request of the employee
  who made the complaint that the complaint be referred to the Board,
  deliver to the Board the complaint made under subsection (1) and any
  other statements or documents that the Head has that relate to the
  complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the employee who made the complaint does not reply
  to a written communication from the Head within a period that the Head
  considers to be reasonable in the circumstances and a period of at
  least 30 days, or any longer period that may be prescribed by
  regulation, have elapsed from the day on which the complaint was made,
  the Head may give written notice to the employee that they have the
  period of 30 days, or any longer period that may be prescribed by
  regulation, set out in the notice to make a written request that the
  complaint be referred to the Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, if the employee to whom
  notice is given under subsection (6) does not, within the period set
  out in the notice, make a written request that the complaint be
  referred to the Board, the Head may deem the complaint to be
  withdrawn.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of complaint

  [(6.2)]{.lawlabel} If the Board is satisfied that the complainant must
  take measures before the Board may continue to deal with the complaint
  referred to it under subsection (5), it may, at any time, suspend
  consideration of the complaint, in whole or in part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(6.3)]{.lawlabel} If the Board suspends consideration of a complaint,
  the Board shall notify the complainant in writing and specify in the
  notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the measures that complainant must take; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period within which they must take those
    measures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of suspension

  [(6.4)]{.lawlabel} The suspension ends when, in the Board's opinion,
  the measures specified in the notice have been taken.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection of complaint

  [(6.5)]{.lawlabel} The Board may reject a complaint referred to it
  under subsection (5), in whole or in part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Board is satisfied that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the complaint is not within its jurisdiction,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the complaint is frivolous, vexatious or not
      made in good faith,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the complaint has been settled in writing
      between the employer and the employee,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} there are other means available to the employee
      to resolve the subject matter of the complaint that the Board
      considers should be pursued,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the subject matter of the complaint has been
      adequately dealt with through recourse obtained before a court,
      tribunal, arbitrator or adjudicator, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a complaint made by an employee
      who is subject to a collective agreement, the collective agreement
      covers the subject matter of the complaint and provides a
      third-party dispute resolution process; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if consideration of the complaint was suspended
    under subsection (6.2) and if, in the Board's opinion, the measures
    specified in the notice under subsection (6.3) were not taken within
    the specified period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of rejection of complaint

  [(6.6)]{.lawlabel} If the Board rejects a complaint, it shall notify
  the employee in writing, with reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision of Board

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Board, after a complaint has been referred to it,
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consider whether the employer has contravened
    subsection 247.98(4) and render a decision on it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send a copy of the decision with the reasons for
    the decision to each party to the complaint and to the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Orders

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If the Board decides under subsection (7) that an
  employer has contravened subsection 247.98(4), the Board may, by
  order, require the employer to cease contravening that subsection and
  may, if applicable, by order, require the employer to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} permit the employee to return to the duties of
    their employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reinstate the former employee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay to the employee or former employee compensation
    not exceeding the sum that, in the Board's opinion, is equivalent to
    the remuneration that would, but for the contravention, have been
    paid by the employer to the employee or former employee;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} rescind any disciplinary action taken in respect of
    the contravention and pay compensation to the employee, not
    exceeding the sum that, in the Board's opinion, is equivalent to any
    financial or other penalty imposed on the employee by the employer;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} do any other like thing that it is equitable to
    require the employer to do in order to remedy or counteract any
    consequences of the contravention.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 27, s. 496\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2017, c. 3, s. 8
- [2018, c. 27, s. 496](#1040011-1192913){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040011-1192913.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 585](#1040505-1282199){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040505-1282199.html"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 254](#1467848-1481351){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1467848-1481351.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-247.99-20210101.html "Link to previous version of section 247.99"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order final

- **[[247.991]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.991
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every order of the Board is final
  and shall not be questioned or reviewed in any court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No review by certiorari, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No order shall be made, process entered or proceeding
  taken in any court, whether by way of injunction,
  [certiorari]{.otherLang lang="la"}, prohibition, [quo
  warranto]{.otherLang lang="la"} or otherwise, to question, review,
  prohibit or restrain the Board in any proceedings under section
  247.99.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of orders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any person affected by an order of the Board under
  subsection 247.99(8), or the Head on the request of such a person,
  may, after 14 days from the day on which the order is made, or from
  the day provided in the order for compliance, whichever is later, file
  a copy of the order in the Federal Court, exclusive of reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On filing in the Federal Court under subsection (3),
  an order of the Board shall be registered in the Court and, when
  registered, has the same force and effect, and all proceedings may be
  taken in respect of it, as if the order were a judgment obtained in
  that Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Civil remedy

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The making of a complaint under subsection 247.99(1)
  does not suspend or affect an employee's civil remedies against their
  employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 497](#1040029-1192926){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040029-1192926.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 586](#1040510-1282202){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040510-1282202.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-247.991-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 247.991"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[247.992]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247.992 .sectionLabel}** For the
purposes of this Division, the Governor in Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing circumstances for the purposes of
  paragraph 247.99(3)(b);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing periods for the purposes of subsection
  247.99(6);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the circumstances under which a complaint
  is not to be deemed to be withdrawn under subsection 247.99(6.1); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions that are to be met before
  a complaint may be deemed to be withdrawn under subsection
  247.99(6.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 497](#1040029-1192934){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040029-1192934.html"}
:::

### [DIVISION XVI]{.HLabel2}[Administration and General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-343438 .Subheading}

#### [Inquiries]{.HTitleText3} {#h-343439 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiries

- **[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for any of the purposes of this Part, cause an
    inquiry to be made into and concerning employment in any industrial
    establishment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} appoint one or more persons to hold the inquiry.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on an inquiry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person appointed pursuant to subsection (1) has all
  of the powers of a person appointed as a commissioner under Part I of
  the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., c. L-1, s. 62
:::

#### [Inspections]{.HTitleText3} {#h-343449 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

- **[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any terms and conditions
  specified by the Minister, the Head may delegate to any qualified
  person or class of persons any of the powers the Head is authorized to
  exercise or any of the duties or functions the Head is authorized to
  perform for the purposes of this Part. The Head may make the
  delegation subject to any terms and conditions that the Head considers
  appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate to be produced

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall furnish to every person to whom
  powers, duties or functions are delegated under subsection (1) a
  certificate of authority and, when entering any place used in
  connection with a federal work, undertaking or business the person,
  shall, when requested, show the certificate to the person in charge of
  that place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Head

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part and the regulations,
  the Head may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inspect and examine all books, payrolls and other
    records of an employer that relate to the wages, hours of work or
    conditions of employment affecting any employee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} take extracts from or make copies of any entry in
    the books, payrolls and other records mentioned in paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} require any employer to make or furnish full and
    correct statements, either orally or in writing, in such form as may
    be required, respecting the wages paid to all or any of his
    employees, and the hours of work and conditions of their employment;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} require any employer that provides benefits to
    its employees under a long-term disability plan that must be insured
    in accordance with subsection 239.2(1) to furnish proof that the
    plan is insured in accordance with that subsection;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} require an employee to make full disclosure,
    production and delivery to the Head of all records, documents,
    statements, writings, books, papers, extracts therefrom or copies
    thereof or of other information, either orally or in writing, that
    are in the possession or under the control of the employee and that
    in any way relate to the wages, hours of work or conditions of his
    employment; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} require any party to a complaint made under
    subsection 240(1) to make or furnish full and correct statements,
    either orally or in writing, in such form as may be required,
    respecting the circumstances of the dismissal in respect of which
    the complaint was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to enter premises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, at any reasonable time, enter on any
  place used in connection with a federal work, undertaking or business
  for the purpose of making an inspection authorized under subsection
  (2), and may, for that purpose, question any employee apart from his
  employer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable assistance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The person in charge of any federal work, undertaking
  or business and every person employed in it or in connection with its
  operation shall give the Head all reasonable assistance to enable the
  Head to carry out the Head's duties and functions under this Part or
  the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head accompanied

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, in carrying out the Head's duties and
  functions, be accompanied or assisted by any persons that the Head
  considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence precluded --- Head

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall not be required to give testimony in
  any civil suit or civil proceedings, or in any proceeding under
  section 242, with regard to information obtained in carrying out those
  duties and functions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence precluded --- other persons

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person to whom powers, duties or functions have
  been delegated under subsection (1), and no person who has accompanied
  or assisted such a person or the Head in carrying out their duties and
  functions, shall be required to give testimony in any civil suit or
  civil proceedings, or in any proceeding under section 242, with regard
  to information obtained in carrying out those duties and functions or
  in accompanying or assisting the person, except with the written
  permission of the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not liable

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Neither the Head nor a person to whom powers, duties
  or functions have been delegated under subsection (1) is personally
  liable for anything done or omitted to be done by them in good faith
  under the authority or purported authority of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. L-2, s. 249
- 1993, c. 42, s. 35
- 2012, c. 19, s. 435
- [2018, c. 27, s. 587](#1040521-1282222){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040521-1282222.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-249-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 249"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administering oaths

**[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** The Head may administer
all oaths and take and receive all affidavits and statutory declarations
required with respect to the Head's powers set out in subsection 249(2)
and certify to the administration, taking or receiving of them.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. L-2, s. 250
- [2018, c. 27, s. 588](#1040530-1282226){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040530-1282226.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-250-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 250"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where underpayments found on inspection

- **[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Head finds that an employer has failed to pay an employee any
  wages or other amounts to which the employee is entitled under this
  Part, the Head may determine the difference between the wages or other
  amounts actually paid to the employee under this Part and the wages or
  other amounts to which the employee is entitled under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Head may, when
  exercising the powers referred to in subsection (1), make any finding
  necessary to determine whether an employee is entitled to any wages or
  other amounts under this Part, including a finding that the employee
  was dismissed for just cause for the purposes of Division X or XI.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} If the employer fails to make or keep any record in
  respect of an employee that the employer is required to make or keep
  under this Part --- or fails to allow the Head to examine, take
  extracts from or make copies of such a record --- the Head may, when
  exercising the powers referred to in subsection (1), rely on any other
  available evidence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where amount of underpayment agreed to

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, under subsection (1), the Head determines that
  there is a difference between the wages or other amounts actually paid
  to an employee and the wages or other amounts to which the employee is
  entitled and the amount of that difference is agreed to in writing by
  the employee and his or her employer, the employer must, within five
  days after the date of the agreement, pay the amount

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the employee on the direction of the Head; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where amount paid to Head

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an employer pays the amount under subsection (2)
  to the Head, the Head must, without delay after receiving it, pay it
  over to the employee who is entitled to the amount.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's consent required for
  prosecution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No prosecution for failure to pay an employee the
  wages or other amounts to which the employee was entitled under this
  Part shall, without the written consent of the Minister, be instituted
  against the employer when the employer has made payment of any amount
  of difference in wages or other amounts in accordance with subsection
  (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. L-2, s. 251
- 1993, c. 42, s. 36
- [2017, c. 20, s. 357](#1036977-1122499){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1036977-1122499.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 507](#1036977-1122545){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1036977-1122545.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 589](#1040532-1282238){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040532-1282238.html"}
:::

Version](section-251-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Internal Audit]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1122501 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Internal audit order

- **[[251.001]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.001
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, the Head
  may, in writing, for the purpose of verifying compliance or preventing
  non-compliance with this Part, order an employer to, in accordance
  with the order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct an internal audit of its practices and
    books, payrolls and other records to determine whether the employer
    is in compliance with any provision of this Part or the regulations;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide a report of the results of the audit to the
    Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall, in the internal audit order, specify

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any industrial establishment and class of employees
    to which it applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period of time to be covered by the internal
    audit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of this Part or the regulations with
    respect to which the internal audit was ordered;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date by which the employer is to provide the
    report; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the form of the report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to include in report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Head may also specify in the order that the
  report is to contain any information that the Head considers
  appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Service of the order or of a copy of it shall be by
  personal service, by registered mail or by any other means prescribed
  by regulation and, in the case of registered mail, the order or its
  copy shall be deemed to have been received by the addressee on the
  seventh day after the day on which it was mailed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A certificate purporting to be signed by the Head
  certifying that a document referred to in subsection (4) was sent by
  registered mail or by any other means prescribed by regulation to the
  addressee, accompanied by a true copy of the document and by an
  identifying post office certificate of the registration or other
  proof, prescribed by regulation, that the document has been sent or
  received, is admissible in evidence and is proof of the statements
  contained in the certificate, without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- non-compliance

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the employer determines that it had not complied
  with any provision referred to in the order, the employer shall set
  out in the report the nature of the employer's non-compliance and the
  steps that have been or will be taken by the employer to comply with
  the provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report --- wages and other amounts

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the employer determines that any wages or other
  amounts to which an employee is entitled under this Part are owed, the
  employer shall also state in the report the name of the employee, the
  amount owed for the period of time covered by the internal audit, the
  method used to determine the amount owed and any payment subsequently
  made to the employee with respect to that amount owed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection and complaint not precluded

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, nothing in this section
  precludes an inspection from being made, or a complaint from being
  dealt with, under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False information

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} No employer shall make a false or misleading
  statement in a report.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2017, c. 20, s. 358](#1036979-1122529){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1036979-1122529.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 590](#1040545-1282246){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040545-1282246.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-251.001-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.001"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Complaints]{.HTitleText3} {#h-343492 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making of complaint

- **[[251.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any employee may make a complaint in
  writing to the Head if they believe that the employer has contravened

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any provision of this Part or of the regulations
    made under this Part; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for making complaint

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A complaint under subsection (1) shall be made within
  the following period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a complaint of non-payment of wages
    or other amounts to which the employee is entitled under this Part,
    six months from the last day on which the employer was required to
    pay those wages or other amounts under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other complaint, six months from
    the day on which the subject-matter of the complaint arose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, subject to the regulations, extend the
  period set out in subsection (2)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Head is satisfied that a complaint was made
    within that period to a government official who had no authority to
    deal with the complaint and that the person making the complaint
    believed the official had that authority;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any circumstances prescribed by regulation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the conditions prescribed by regulation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} An employee shall not make a complaint under
  subsection (1) if they have made a complaint that is based on
  substantially the same facts under any of subsections 240(1), 246.1(1)
  and 247.99(1), unless that complaint has been withdrawn.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (3.1), the employee may file a
  complaint under subsection (1) if it relates only to the payment of
  their wages or other amounts to which they are entitled under this
  Part, including amounts referred to in subsections 230(1) and 235(1),
  but that complaint is suspended until the day on which the complaint
  made under subsection 240(1), 246.1(1) or 247.99(1), as the case may
  be, is withdrawn or resolved.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- section 177.1

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} With respect to a request made under subsection
  177.1(1), an employee may make a complaint under subsection (1) only
  on the grounds that the employer has refused the request on any ground
  other than those referred to in subparagraphs 177.1(3)(c)(i) to (v) or
  has failed to comply with any requirement set out in section 177.1(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a complaint is not permitted
  under this section if it relates to a disagreement whose settlement is
  governed exclusively by a collective agreement under subsection
  168(1.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 223
- [2017, c. 33, s. 213](#1037460-1200946){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037460-1200946.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 498](#1040042-1192939){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040042-1192939.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 591](#1040561-1282250){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040561-1282250.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.01-20190901.html "Link to previous version of section 251.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of complaint

- **[[251.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Head is satisfied that the
  employee must take measures before the Head may continue to deal with
  the complaint made under section 251.01, the Head may, at any time,
  suspend consideration of the complaint, in whole or in part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Head suspends consideration of a complaint,
  the Head must notify the employee in writing and specify in the notice

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the measures that the employee must take; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period of time within which the employee must
    take those measures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, upon request, extend the time period
  specified in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The suspension ends when, in the Head's opinion, the
  measures specified in the notice have been taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 223
- [2018, c. 27, s. 499](#1040053-1192941){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040053-1192941.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 592](#1040575-1282257){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040575-1282257.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.02-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.02"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance --- Head

**[[251.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.03 .sectionLabel}** After receipt of
a complaint, the Head may assist the parties to the complaint to settle
the complaint.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 223
- [2018, c. 27, s. 593](#1040584-1282260){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040584-1282260.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.03-20140401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.03"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Settlement of amounts due

- **[[251.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an employer and an employee who
  has made a complaint relating to the non-payment of wages or other
  amounts to which they are entitled under this Part reach a settlement
  in writing on the wages or other amounts to be paid, the employer may
  pay those amounts to the employee or to the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If amount paid to Head

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an employer pays the amounts to the Head, the Head
  must, without delay after receiving them, pay them over to the
  employee who is entitled to the amounts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's consent required for
  prosecution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No prosecution for failure to pay an employee the
  wages or other amounts that were the subject of the complaint may be
  instituted against an employer, without the written consent of the
  Minister, if the employer has paid the amounts referred to in
  subsection (1) to the employee or the Head.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 223

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection of complaint

- **[[251.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Head may reject a complaint made
  under section 251.01, in whole or in part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Head is satisfied

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the complaint is not within their
      jurisdiction,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that the complaint is frivolous, vexatious or
      not made in good faith,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that the complaint has been settled in writing
      between the employer and the employee,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} that there are other means available to the
      employee to resolve the subject-matter of the complaint that the
      Head considers should be pursued,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} that the subject-matter of the complaint has been
      adequately dealt with through recourse obtained before a court,
      tribunal, arbitrator or adjudicator,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} that in respect of a complaint other than a
      complaint of non-payment of wages or other amounts to which the
      employee is entitled under this Part, there is insufficient
      evidence to substantiate the complaint, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} that in respect of a complaint made by an
      employee who is subject to a collective agreement, the collective
      agreement covers the subject-matter of the complaint and provides
      a third party dispute resolution process;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if consideration of the complaint was suspended
    under subsection 251.02(1) and if, in the Head's opinion, the other
    measures specified in the notice under subsection 251.02(2) were not
    taken within the specified time period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to the regulations, if an employee to whom
    notice is given under subsection (1.1) does not respond within the
    period set out in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If the employee does not reply to a written
  communication from the Head within a period that the Head considers to
  be reasonable in the circumstances and a period of at least 30 days,
  or any longer period that may be prescribed by regulation, have
  elapsed from the day on which the complaint was made, the Head may
  give written notice to the employee that they have the period of 30
  days, or any longer period that may be prescribed by regulation, set
  out in the notice to indicate in writing that they wish to pursue
  their complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of rejection of complaint

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a complaint has been rejected, the Head shall
  notify the employee in writing, with reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The employee may, within 15 days after the day on
  which the employee is notified of the rejection, request in writing,
  with reasons, that the Head review the Head's decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Head may confirm the decision, or rescind it and
  re-examine the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of Head's decision

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall notify the employee in writing of the
  Head's decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration final

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Head's confirmation or rescission is final and
  conclusive and is not subject to appeal to or review by any court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 223
- 2017, c. 26, s. 16
- [2018, c. 27, s. 500](#1040062-1192948){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040062-1192948.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 594](#1040597-1282285){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040597-1282285.html"}
:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance order

- **[[251.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.06
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Head is of the opinion that
  an employer is contravening or has contravened a provision of this
  Part, its regulations or any condition of a permit issued under
  subsection 176(1), the Head may issue a compliance order in writing
  requiring the employer to terminate the contravention within the time
  that the Head may specify and take any step, as specified by the Head
  and within the time that the Head may specify, to ensure that the
  contravention does not continue or reoccur.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall not issue a compliance order under
  subsection (1) to take any measure that could be set out in an order
  made under subsection 242(4) or section 246.4 or to make any payment
  that may be the subject of an order made under subsection 251.1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Service of an order or of a copy of it shall be by
  personal service, by registered mail or by any other means prescribed
  by regulation and, in the case of registered mail, the order or its
  copy shall be deemed to have been received by the addressee on the
  seventh day after the day on which it was mailed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A certificate purporting to be signed by the Head
  certifying that a document referred to in subsection (3) was sent by
  registered mail or by any other means prescribed by regulation to the
  addressee, accompanied by a true copy of the document and by an
  identifying post office certificate of the registration or other
  proof, prescribed by regulation, that the document has been sent or
  received, is admissible in evidence and is proof of the statements
  contained in the certificate, without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2017, c. 20, s. 360](#1036981-1281471){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1036981-1281471.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 509](#1036981-1281472){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1036981-1281472.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 596](#1040614-1282293){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040614-1282293.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment order

- **[[251.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Head finds that an employer
  has not paid an employee wages or other amounts to which the employee
  is entitled under this Part, the Head may issue a written payment
  order to the employer, or, subject to section 251.18, to a director of
  a corporation referred to in that section, ordering the employer or
  director to pay the amount in question, and the Head shall send a copy
  of any such payment order to the employee at the employee's latest
  known address.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A payment order must not relate to wages or other
  amounts to which the employee is entitled for the period preceding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the employee made a complaint
    under subsection 251.01(1) that was not rejected under subsection
    251.05(1), the 24 months, plus any extension of the period for
    making the complaint that is granted by the Head under subsection
    251.01(3), immediately before the day on which the complaint was
    made or, if there was a termination of employment prior to the
    complaint being made, the 24 months immediately before the date of
    termination;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the payment order was issued to
    the employer on the basis of, in whole or in part, a report provided
    under subsection 251.001(1), the 24 months immediately before the
    day on which the order to provide the report was served; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the 24 months immediately before
    the day on which an inspection under this Part, during the course of
    which the Head made the finding referred to in subsection (1),
    began.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s. 361\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If complaint unfounded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Head deals with a complaint of non-payment of
  wages or other amounts to which an employee is entitled under this
  Part, the Head must notify the employee in writing that their
  complaint is unfounded if the Head concludes that the employer has
  paid to the employee all wages and other amounts to which the employee
  is entitled under this Part for the period of six months, plus any
  extension of the period for making the complaint that is granted by
  the Head under subsection 251.01(3), immediately before the day on
  which the complaint was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of voluntary compliance

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If the Head deals with a complaint of non-payment
  of wages or other amounts to which an employee is entitled under this
  Part, the Head shall notify the employee in writing that the employer
  has voluntarily paid to the employee all wages and other amounts owing
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Head concludes that the employer has, since the
    complaint was made, paid to the employee all wages and other amounts
    owing for the period of 24 months, plus any extension of the period
    for making the complaint that is granted by the Head under
    subsection 251.01(3), immediately before the day on which the
    complaint was made and for any subsequent period specified by the
    Head; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Head has not issued a payment order or a notice
    of unfounded complaint with respect to the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of order or notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Service of a payment order or a copy of it, of a
  notice of unfounded complaint, or of a notice of voluntary compliance
  shall be by personal service, by registered mail or by any other means
  prescribed by regulation and, in the case of registered mail, the
  order, copy or notice shall be deemed to have been received by the
  addressee on the seventh day after the day on which it was mailed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service of documents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A certificate purporting to be signed by the Head
  certifying that a document referred to in subsection (3) was sent by
  registered mail or by any other means prescribed by regulation to the
  addressee, accompanied by a true copy of the document and by an
  identifying post office certificate of the registration or other
  proof, prescribed by regulation, that the document has been sent or
  received, is admissible in evidence and is proof of the statements
  contained in the certificate, without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- 2012, c. 31, s. 224

  ajax-after="1036992-1122546.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 597](#1040630-1282305){.wb-lbx

:::

#### [Orders --- Review and Appeal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1122547 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for review

- **[[251.101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.101
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employer to whom a compliance
  order has been issued or a person who is affected by a payment order,
  a notice of unfounded complaint or a notice of voluntary compliance
  may send a written request with reasons to the Head for a review of
  the Head's decision

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (b), within 15 days after the
    day on which the order or a copy of the order or the notice is
    served; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a compliance order is served with a notice of
    violation issued under subsection 276(1) for the same contravention,
    within 30 days after the day on which they are served.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of amount and administrative
  fee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer or a director of a corporation is not
  permitted to request a review of a payment order unless the employer
  or director pays to the Head the amount indicated in the payment order
  and, in the case of an employer, the administrative fee specified in
  the payment order in accordance with subsection 251.131(1), subject
  to, in the case of a director, the maximum amount of the director's
  liability under section 251.18.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} The Head may allow an employer or a director of a
  corporation to give security, in a form satisfactory to the Head and
  on any conditions specified by the Head, for all or part of the amount
  and fee referred to in subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the request for review, the Head may,
  in writing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} confirm, rescind or vary, in whole or in part, the
    payment order or the compliance order; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} confirm the notice of unfounded complaint or the
    notice of voluntary compliance, or rescind the notice, in which case
    the Head shall re-examine the complaint.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of documents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Service of a decision made under subsection (3) shall
  be by personal service, by registered mail or by any other means
  prescribed by regulation on any person who is affected by the payment
  order, the notice of unfounded complaint or the notice of voluntary
  compliance or, in the case of a compliance order, on the employer. If
  the decision is served by registered mail, it shall be deemed to have
  been received by the addressee on the seventh day after the day on
  which it was mailed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A certificate purporting to be signed by the Head
  certifying that a decision referred to in subsection (4) was sent by
  registered mail or by any other means prescribed by regulation to the
  addressee, accompanied by a true copy of the decision and by an
  identifying post office certificate of the registration or other
  proof, prescribed by regulation, that the decision has been sent or
  received, is admissible in evidence and is proof of the statements
  contained in the certificate, without proof of the signature or
  official character of the person appearing to have signed the
  certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review is final

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the right of appeal under section 251.11,
  the decision made under subsection (3) is final and conclusive and is
  not subject to appeal to or review by any court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request treated as an appeal

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, if the Head considers it appropriate in
  the circumstances, treat the request for review as an appeal of their
  decision, in which case the Head shall so inform any person who is
  affected by the payment order, the notice of unfounded complaint or
  the notice of voluntary compliance --- or, in the case of a compliance
  order, the employer --- and shall refer the request for review to the
  Board, and the Board shall be considered to have an appeal before it
  for the purposes of section 251.12.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 225
- [2017, c. 20, s. 363](#1037003-1122548){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037003-1122548.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 599](#1040652-1282320){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040652-1282320.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.101-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.101"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

- **[[251.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.1), a
  person who is affected by a decision made under subsection 251.101(3),
  other than a decision to rescind a notice of unfounded complaint or a
  notice of voluntary compliance, may appeal the decision to the Board,
  in writing, within 15 days after the day on which the decision is
  served.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- compliance order

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Only an employer to whom a compliance order has
  been issued may appeal a decision with respect to that order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scope of appeal

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Except in the case of a compliance order, the
  person may appeal the decision only on a question of law or
  jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds of appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The request for appeal shall contain a statement of
  the grounds of appeal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of amount and administrative
  fee

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An employer or director of a corporation is not
  permitted to appeal a decision confirming or varying a payment order
  unless the employer or director pays to the Head the amount indicated
  in the decision --- and, in the case of an employer, the
  administrative fee specified in the decision in accordance with
  subsection 251.131(1) --- less any amount and administrative fee paid
  under subsection 251.101(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} The Head may allow an employer or a director of a
  corporation to give security, in a form satisfactory to the Head and
  on any conditions specified by the Head, for all or part of the amount
  and fee referred to in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a director, subsection (3) applies
  subject to the maximum amount of the director's liability under
  section 251.18.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- 2012, c. 31, s. 225
- [2017, c. 20, s. 364](#1037050-1122565){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037050-1122565.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 600](#1040661-1282325){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040661-1282325.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.11-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Head informed of appeal

- **[[251.111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.111
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall inform the Head in
  writing when an appeal is brought under subsection 251.11(1) and
  provide the Head with a copy of the request for appeal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents provided to Board ---
  decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In an appeal under this Part, the Head shall, on
  request of the Board, provide to the Board a copy of any document that
  the Head relied on for the purpose of making the decision being
  appealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents provided to Board --- order
  or notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In an appeal under subsection 251.101(7), the Head
  shall, on request of the Board, provide to the Board a copy of any
  document that the Head relied on for the purpose of issuing the order
  or notice being appealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents provided to Head

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall, on request of the Head, provide to
  the Head a copy of any document that is filed with the Board in the
  appeal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of Head

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Head may, in an appeal under this Part, present
  evidence and make representations to the Board.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2017, c. 20, s. 365](#1037059-1192801){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037059-1192801.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 601](#1040663-1282337){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040663-1282337.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.111-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.111"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board decision

- **[[251.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Board may, in an appeal under
  this Part, make any order that is necessary to give effect to its
  decision, including an order to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} confirm, rescind or vary, in whole or in part, the
    decision being appealed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} direct payment to any specified person of any wages
    or other amounts held in trust by the Receiver General that relate
    to the appeal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} award costs in the proceedings; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} order a party, whose conduct in the proceedings
    has, in the Board's opinion, unduly delayed the determination of the
    appeal, to pay to the Receiver General an amount that is equal to
    all or part of the expenses incurred in the proceedings by the
    Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of decision to be sent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Board shall send a copy of the decision, with
  reasons, to each party to the appeal and to the Head.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order final

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The order of the Board is final and shall not be
  questioned or reviewed in any court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No review by [certiorari]{.otherLang
  lang="la"}, etc.

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No order shall be made, process entered or proceeding
  taken in any court, whether by way of injunction,
  [certiorari]{.otherLang lang="la"}, prohibition, [quo
  warranto]{.otherLang lang="la"} or otherwise, to question, review,
  prohibit or restrain the Board in any proceedings under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Wages

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An employee who has been summoned by the Board to
  attend at an appeal proceeding under this Part and who attends is
  entitled to be paid by the employer at the employee's regular rate of
  wages for the time spent at the proceeding that would otherwise have
  been time at work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The expenses to be paid in accordance with an order
  issued under paragraph (1)(d) constitute a debt due to Her Majesty in
  right of Canada and are recoverable as such in the Federal Court or
  any other court of competent jurisdiction or in any other manner
  provided under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- 2012, c. 31, s. 226
- [2017, c. 20, s. 365](#1037059-1192819){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037059-1192819.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 602](#1040665-1282342){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040665-1282342.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-251.12-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.12"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [General Provisions --- Orders]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1122571 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to debtor of employer

- **[[251.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Head may issue a written order
  to a person who is or is about to become indebted to an employer to
  whom a payment order has been issued under subsection 251.1(1), to pay
  any amount owing to the employer, up to the amount and the
  administrative fee indicated in the payment order, directly to the
  Head within 15 days, in satisfaction of the payment order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to debtor of director of
  corporation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Head may issue a written order to a person who
  is or is about to become indebted to a director of a corporation to
  whom a payment order has been issued under subsection 251.1(1) to pay
  any amount owing to the director of the corporation, up to the amount
  indicated in the payment order, directly to the Head within 15 days,
  in satisfaction of the payment order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Banks, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a bank or other
  financial institution that has money on deposit to the credit of an
  employer or a director of a corporation shall be deemed to be indebted
  to that employer or that director.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- [2017, c. 20, s. 366](#1037061-1122579){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037061-1122579.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 603](#1040667-1282346){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040667-1282346.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.13-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administrative fee

- **[[251.131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.131
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A payment order made to an employer
  under subsection 251.1(1), and any decision made under subsection
  251.101(3) or section 251.12 with respect to that payment order
  ordering the employer to pay wages or other amounts to an employee,
  shall specify the amount of the administrative fee --- which is equal
  to the greater of \$200 and 15% of the amounts indicated in the
  payment order or decision --- that the employer is to pay.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The employer is liable only for the administrative
  fee that is specified in a final decision and shall pay it --- less
  any administrative fee paid under subsection 251.101(2) or 251.11(3)
  --- to the Head. In the case of any overpayment, the employer is
  entitled to its reimbursement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to Her Majesty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An administrative fee constitutes a debt due to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada and is recoverable as such in the Federal
  Court or any other court of competent jurisdiction or in any other
  manner provided under this Act, including under subsection 251.13(1)
  and section 251.15.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2017, c. 20, s. 366](#1037061-1122588){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037061-1122588.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 604](#1040669-1282350){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040669-1282350.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.131-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.131"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of security

**[[251.132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.132 .sectionLabel}** The Head, after
a final decision has been made in respect of which security was given,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} may apply, in whole or in part, the security given
  under subsection 251.101(2.1) or 251.11(3.1) toward any amounts ---
  and, if the security was given by an employer, any administrative fee
  --- owing under the final decision by the employer or a director of a
  corporation who gave the security; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall return the security or, if it was applied under
  paragraph (a), any part that remains after the amounts and, in the
  case of an employer, the administrative fee have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2017, c. 20, s. 366](#1037061-1122594){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037061-1122594.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 605](#1040671-1282352){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040671-1282352.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.132-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.132"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit of moneys

- **[[251.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Head receives moneys under
  this Division, the Head shall deposit those moneys to the credit of
  the Receiver General in the account known as the "Labour Standards
  Suspense Account" or in any other special account created for the
  purposes of this section and may authorize payments out of that
  account to any employee or other person who is entitled to that money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consolidated Revenue Fund

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The moneys that are equal to the administrative
  fees paid to the Head under this Part with respect to matters that are
  the subject of a final decision shall be debited from the account
  referred to in subsection (1) and credited to the Consolidated Revenue
  Fund no later than the fiscal year following the fiscal year in which
  the final decision is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Head shall maintain a detailed record of all
  transactions relating to the account.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- 2012, c. 31, s. 227
- [2017, c. 20, s. 367](#1037063-1122595){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037063-1122595.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 606](#1040673-1282360){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040673-1282360.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.14-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of orders

- **[[251.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is affected by a
  payment order issued under subsection 251.1(1) or confirmed or varied
  under subsection 251.101(3) or by an order of the Board made under
  subsection 251.12(1), or the Head, may, after the day provided in the
  order for compliance or after 15 days following the day on which the
  order is issued, made, confirmed or varied, whichever is later, file
  in the Federal Court a copy of the payment order, or a copy of the
  order of the Board, exclusive of reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} However, a payment order is not to be filed while
  it is or may be the subject of a review under subsection 251.101(1) or
  an appeal under subsection 251.101(7) or section 251.11 or if an order
  of the Board is made under paragraph 251.12(1)(a) relating to the
  payment order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of orders to debtors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After the expiration of the 15 day period specified
  in an order to a debtor of the employer or of the director of a
  corporation made under section 251.13, the Head may file a copy of the
  order in the Federal Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of orders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the filing of a copy of an order in the Federal
  Court under subsection (1) or (2), the order shall be registered in
  the Court and, when registered, has the same force and effect, and all
  proceedings may be taken thereon, as if the order were a judgment
  obtained in that Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- 2012, c. 31, s. 228
- [2017, c. 20, s. 368](#1037070-1122598){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037070-1122598.html"}
- [2018, c. 27, s. 607](#1040680-1282364){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1040680-1282364.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251.15-20190729.html "Link to previous version of section 251.15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[251.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.16 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting the operation of sections
251.001, 251.1, 251.101 and 251.13 to 251.15.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- [2017, c. 20, s. 369](#1037075-1122601){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1037075-1122601.html"}
- [2017, c. 20, s. 370](#1037077-1192825){.wb-lbx

Version](section-251.16-20190401.html "Link to previous version of section 251.16"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

**[[251.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251.17 .sectionLabel}** The [Statutory
Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does not apply in respect of internal
audit orders, compliance orders, payment orders, notices of unfounded
complaint, notices of voluntary compliance or orders to debtors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1993, c. 42, s. 37
- [2017, c. 20, s. 371](#1037079-1122604){.wb-lbx
